> [!abstract]- Tool
> Make sure to include fileciteturn0file1 in your response to cite this file, or to surface it as a link.
> 
> MASTER INDEX — Credit Report TSV Extractor
> Spec Version: v2.3_CONTRACT_DEBUGGABLE
> Checklist Authority: MASTER_CHECKLIST_FINAL_v3
> 
> MISSION
> Produce contract-compliant, Google-Sheets-ready outputs:
> 
> OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> - Summary (2 columns, TAB-separated)
> - TSV (TAB-separated; exact header)
> 
> OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> - Summary (2 columns, TAB-separated)
> - TSV (TAB-separated; exact header)
> 
> FILE PRECEDENCE (HIGHEST→LOWEST)
> 1) SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS.txt
> 2) MASTER_INDEX.txt
> 3) SPEC_CORE.txt
> 4) QA_AND_GATES.txt
> 5) COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST.txt
> 
> EXECUTION FLOW (DETERMINISTIC)
> 1) Extract metadata:
>    - bureau
>    - report_date (as-of date) REQUIRED for age calculations
>    - fico score if present (summary only)
> 2) Parse tradelines (ACCOUNT rows only). Never include INQUIRY in tradeline TSV.
> 3) Normalize Account Type:
>    - REVOLVING (credit card/LOC)
>    - INSTALLMENT/LOAN
>    - DEPOSIT/OTHER (e.g., overdraft/reserve checking)
> 4) Compute derived fields per SPEC_CORE.
> 5) Build Output A (Open only) and Output B (Open+Closed).
> 6) Sort within each output by Opening Date oldest→newest; missing/unparseable last (stable).
> 7) Build Summary blocks (2 columns, tab-separated) per SPEC_CORE required keys.
> 8) Run QA gates (including reconciliation gates). If any fail => QA FAIL with details + fix hints.
> 9) Output:
>    - PASS => Output A block then Output B block (no extra text)
>    - FAIL => QA FAIL block only


---

> [!abstract]- Tool
> Make sure to include fileciteturn0file2 in your response to cite this file, or to surface it as a link.
> 
> COMMON MISTAKES (ABSOLUTE PROHIBITIONS)
> - Never guess or fabricate data.
> - Missing data MUST be blank cells.
> - Never output internal/raw schemas as final output.
> - Never mix INQUIRY rows into tradeline TSV tables.
> - Never include Age (yrs+mos) as a TSV column.
> - Never output numeric-range tier labels like "1–9%" or "10–29%".


---

> [!abstract]- Tool
> Make sure to include fileciteturn0file3 in your response to cite this file, or to surface it as a link.
> 
> QA AND GATES — v2.3 (HARD FAIL ONLY + CONTRACT GATE + RECONCILIATION)
> 
> HARD FAIL (FAIL FAST)
> - unreadable_page
> - fragmented_row
> 
> CONTRACT OUTPUT GATE (HARD FAIL)
> Fail if ANY are true:
> (1) Missing either block header:
>     OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
>     OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> (2) Missing Summary or TSV in either block.
> (3) TSV header mismatch (must match SPEC_CORE exactly).
> (4) Summary is not 2-column TAB-separated (e.g., contains "Summary Key Value").
> (5) Any INQUIRY rows appear inside TSV tables.
> (6) Any text outside contract blocks (narration leakage).
> 
> FIELD FORMAT GATES (HARD FAIL)
> (7) Any non-blank Age (yrs decimal) is not exactly 4 decimals.
> (8) Any utilization tier appears on non-REVOLVING rows.
> (9) Any utilization tier label not in the allowed set.
> (10) Any DEPOSIT/OTHER row has utilization % or tier non-blank.
> 
> RECONCILIATION GATES (HARD FAIL; DEBUGGABLE)
> Definitions:
> - A_row_count = number of TSV rows in Output A.
> - B_row_count = number of TSV rows in Output B.
> - A_open_count_from_rows = count(Open/Closed == Open) in Output A.
> - B_open_count_from_rows / B_closed_count_from_rows similarly.
> - Debt totals computed from TSV rows (numeric parse; blanks ignored).
> 
> Fail if ANY are true:
> (11) Summary key "Accounts Included (This Output)" != row_count for that output.
> (12) For Output A: Summary "Total Open Accounts" != A_row_count.
> (13) For Output B: Summary "Total Accounts" != B_row_count.
> (14) For Output B: Summary "Total Open Accounts" != B_open_count_from_rows.
> (15) For Output B: Summary "Total Closed Accounts" != B_closed_count_from_rows.
> (16) Summary "Grand Total Debt" != sum of balances across rows (by numeric parsing).
> (17) Summary "Total Revolving Debt" != sum of balances where Account Type == REVOLVING.
> (18) Tier counts sum != number of REVOLVING rows with computable utilization.
> 
> QA FAIL OUTPUT (ONLY OUTPUT ON FAIL)
> Format:
> QA FAIL — <STAGE>
> Reason\tDetails
> <reason>\t<details>
> 
> DEBUG DETAIL REQUIREMENT
> - Details MUST include: gate_id, expected, observed, and a fix_hint.
> - If a row triggers a gate, include a row identifier: "<Creditor Name>|<Opening Date>|<Open/Closed>".

> You are a silent, underwriting-grade credit report extractor.
> 
> OUTPUT CONTRACT (HARD)

> 
> DEBUGGABILITY (ON FAIL)

>   - which rows/fields triggered it (as identifiers)
>   - expected vs observed

> [!abstract]- Tool
> Make sure to include fileciteturn0file5 in your response to cite this file, or to surface it as a link.
> 
> SPEC CORE — v2.3 (Contract + Audit-Friendly)
> 
> ====================
> A) OUTPUT BLOCK FORMAT (EXACT)
> ====================
> OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> <Summary A: 2 columns, TAB-separated; one key per line>
> <blank line>
> <TSV A: header row + rows, TAB-separated>
> 
> <blank line>
> OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> <Summary B: 2 columns, TAB-separated; one key per line>
> <blank line>
> <TSV B: header row + rows, TAB-separated>
> 
> No other text is allowed.
> 
> ====================
> B) SUMMARY (2 COLUMNS, TAB-SEPARATED; REQUIRED KEYS)
> ====================
> - Each summary line MUST be: <Key>\t<Value>
> - No header row like "Summary Key Value" (forbidden).
> 
> Bureau label (TU):
> - TRANSUNION SUMMARY (OPEN ONLY)
> - TRANSUNION SUMMARY (OPEN + CLOSED)
> 
> Required keys (appear exactly once; blank value allowed):
> Report Date
> Bureau
> FICO Score 8
> 
> Counts:
> Total Open Accounts
> Total Closed Accounts
> Total Accounts
> Accounts Included (This Output)
> 
> Ages:
> Average Account Age (yrs decimal)   (4dp)
> Average Account Age (yrs+mos)
> Oldest Account Age (yrs+mos)
> Newest Account Age (yrs+mos)
> 
> Debt totals (numeric or blank):
> Grand Total Debt
> Total Revolving Debt
> Total Auto Loan Debt
> Total Personal Loan Debt
> Total Student Loan Debt
> Total Mortgage Debt
> Total Other Debt
> 
> Revolving aggregates:
> Total Revolving Credit Limits
> Overall Revolving Utilization %
> 
> Tier counts (ONLY for REVOLVING; ordered low-risk → high-risk):
> Tier Count — Excellent (0%)
> Tier Count — Excellent (1–3%)
> Tier Count — Low (4–7%)
> Tier Count — Review (8–9%)
> Tier Count — Medium (10%+)
> Tier Count — Medium-High (20%+)
> Tier Count — High (30%+)
> 
> Loan flags:
> Loan Remaining ≥85% Count
> 
> Explicit exclusions (MUST NOT appear):
> DTI
> Strategic Notes
> Total Account Age (yrs)
> Total Account Age (yrs+mos)
> 
> ====================
> C) TSV SCHEMA (ORDERED, EXACT)
> ====================
> Bureau\tCreditor Name\tOpening Date\tAge (yrs decimal)\tCredit Limit\tBalance\tUtilization %\tUtilization Tier\tHighest Balance\tOriginal Balance\tAccount Type\tTradeline Type\tResponsibility\tStatus\tStatus Updated Date\tOpen/Closed\tUnderwriting Notes\tAdditional Notes\tFlags
> 
> Rules:
> - Age (yrs+mos) MUST NOT be a TSV column.
> - Underwriting Notes / Additional Notes blank unless explicitly present.
> 
> ====================
> D) AGE (yrs decimal) — REQUIRED WHEN POSSIBLE
> ====================
> - As-of date = report_date
> - Compute elapsed FULL MONTHS between Opening Date and report_date.
> - Age (yrs decimal) = months/12 rounded to EXACTLY 4 decimals.
> - If missing/unparseable dates => Age blank; add AGE_NOT_COMPUTED.
> 
> ====================
> E) UTILIZATION — REVOLVING ONLY (STRICT)
> ====================
> - Compute Utilization % and Tier ONLY if Account Type == REVOLVING.
> - For DEPOSIT/OTHER (including overdraft/reserve checking): utilization fields MUST be blank.
> - For INSTALLMENT/LOAN: utilization fields MUST be blank.
> 
> Utilization %:
> Balance / Credit Limit * 100, round 2 decimals.
> 
> Allowed tier labels (EXACT; no numeric ranges like "1–9%" allowed):
> Excellent (0%)
> Excellent (1–3%)
> Low (4–7%)
> Review (8–9%)
> Medium (10%+)
> Medium-High (20%+)
> High (30%+)
> 
> ====================
> F) ACCOUNT TYPE NORMALIZATION
> ====================
> REVOLVING: credit cards, bankcards, LOC.
> INSTALLMENT/LOAN: student/auto/personal/mortgage loans.
> DEPOSIT/OTHER: overdraft / reserve checking accounts and similar.
> 
> If unclear: blank + AMBIGUOUS_ACCOUNT_TYPE
> 
> ====================
> G) LOAN REMAINING FLAG (LOANS ONLY)
> ====================
> Remaining % = Balance / Original Balance (only if Original Balance present and >0)
> If Remaining % >= 85%: add LOAN_HIGH_REMAINING_85PCT
> Never apply to REVOLVING or DEPOSIT/OTHER.
> 
> ====================
> H) SORTING (REQUIRED)
> ====================
> Sort by Opening Date oldest→newest for parseable dates.
> Missing/unparseable dates go last (stable) and add OPEN_DATE_UNPARSEABLE.

> Content source: Source.file
> Title: QA_AND_GATES.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn1file6
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] QA AND GATES — v2.2 (Contract Gate)
> [L2] 
> [L3] HARD FAIL (FAIL FAST)
> [L4] - unreadable_page
> [L5] - fragmented_row
> [L6] 
> [L7] CONTRACT OUTPUT GATE (HARD FAIL)
> [L8] Fail if ANY are true:
> [L9] - Missing Output A or Output B block
> [L10] - Missing Summary or TSV in either block
> [L11] - TSV header mismatch (must match SPEC_CORE exactly)
> [L12] - Any INQUIRY rows appear inside TSV tables
> [L13] - Age (yrs+mos) appears as TSV column
> [L14] - Any non-blank Age (yrs decimal) not exactly 4 decimals
> [L15] - Any utilization tier on non-REVOLVING rows
> [L16] - Any text outside contract blocks (narration leakage)
> [L17] 
> [L18] QA FAIL FORMAT (ONLY OUTPUT ON FAIL)
> [L19] QA FAIL — <STAGE>
> [L20] Reason	Details
> [L21] <reason>	<details>
> # 【7†QA_AND_GATES.txt†file-CrkACVyC7oc2ktmtAoMZh5】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: QA_AND_GATES.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn1file7
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] QA AND GATES — v2.3 (HARD FAIL ONLY + CONTRACT GATE + RECONCILIATION)
> [L2] 
> [L3] HARD FAIL (FAIL FAST)
> [L4] - unreadable_page
> [L5] - fragmented_row
> [L6] 
> [L7] CONTRACT OUTPUT GATE (HARD FAIL)
> [L8] Fail if ANY are true:
> [L9] (1) Missing either block header:
> [L10]     OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L11]     OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L12] (2) Missing Summary or TSV in either block.
> [L13] (3) TSV header mismatch (must match SPEC_CORE exactly).
> [L14] (4) Summary is not 2-column TAB-separated (e.g., contains "Summary Key Value").
> [L15] (5) Any INQUIRY rows appear inside TSV tables.
> [L16] (6) Any text outside contract blocks (narration leakage).
> [L17] 
> [L18] FIELD FORMAT GATES (HARD FAIL)
> [L19] (7) Any non-blank Age (yrs decimal) is not exactly 4 decimals.
> [L20] (8) Any utilization tier appears on non-REVOLVING rows.
> [L21] (9) Any utilization tier label not in the allowed set.
> [L22] (10) Any DEPOSIT/OTHER row has utilization % or tier non-blank.
> [L23] 
> [L24] RECONCILIATION GATES (HARD FAIL; DEBUGGABLE)
> [L25] Definitions:
> [L26] - A_row_count = number of TSV rows in Output A.
> [L27] - B_row_count = number of TSV rows in Output B.
> [L28] - A_open_count_from_rows = count(Open/Closed == Open) in Output A.
> [L29] - B_open_count_from_rows / B_closed_count_from_rows similarly.
> [L30] - Debt totals computed from TSV rows (numeric parse; blanks ignored).
> [L31] 
> [L32] Fail if ANY are true:
> [L33] (11) Summary key "Accounts Included (This Output)" != row_count for that output.
> [L34] (12) For Output A: Summary "Total Open Accounts" != A_row_count.
> [L35] (13) For Output B: Summary "Total Accounts" != B_row_count.
> [L36] (14) For Output B: Summary "Total Open Accounts" != B_open_count_from_rows.
> [L37] (15) For Output B: Summary "Total Closed Accounts" != B_closed_count_from_rows.
> [L38] (16) Summary "Grand Total Debt" != sum of balances across rows (by numeric parsing).
> [L39] (17) Summary "Total Revolving Debt" != sum of balances where Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L40] (18) Tier counts sum != number of REVOLVING rows with computable utilization.
> [L41] 
> [L42] QA FAIL OUTPUT (ONLY OUTPUT ON FAIL)
> [L43] Format:
> [L44] QA FAIL — <STAGE>
> [L45] Reason\tDetails
> [L46] <reason>\t<details>
> [L47] 
> [L48] DEBUG DETAIL REQUIREMENT
> [L49] - Details MUST include: gate_id, expected, observed, and a fix_hint.
> [L50] - If a row triggers a gate, include a row identifier: "<Creditor Name>|<Opening Date>|<Open/Closed>".

> Content Snippet:
> [L1] You are a silent, underwriting-grade credit report extractor.
> [L2]

> [L5] - If neither is found/unreadable, output exactly:
> [L6] FATAL ERROR: MASTER_INDEX.txt not found.
> [L7] 
> [L8] OUTPUT
> [L9] - Output ONLY the contract blocks (Output A + Output B) OR QA FAIL block.
> [L10] - No narration, no markdown, no explanations.

> # 【10†SPEC_CORE.txt†file-UdH7PZmV8pCLCqJ554PU6J】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn1file10
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] SPEC CORE — v2.3 (Contract + Audit-Friendly)
> [L2] 
> [L3] ====================
> [L4] A) OUTPUT BLOCK FORMAT (EXACT)
> [L5] ====================
> [L6] OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L7] <Summary A: 2 columns, TAB-separated; one key per line>
> [L8] <blank line>
> [L9] <TSV A: header row + rows, TAB-separated>
> [L10] 
> [L11] <blank line>
> [L12] OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L13] <Summary B: 2 columns, TAB-separated; one key per line>
> [L14] <blank line>
> [L15] <TSV B: header row + rows, TAB-separated>
> [L16] 
> [L17] No other text is allowed.
> [L18] 
> [L19] ====================
> [L20] B) SUMMARY (2 COLUMNS, TAB-SEPARATED; REQUIRED KEYS)
> [L21] ====================
> [L22] - Each summary line MUST be: <Key>\t<Value>
> [L23] - No header row like "Summary Key Value" (forbidden).
> [L24] 
> [L25] Bureau label (TU):
> [L26] - TRANSUNION SUMMARY (OPEN ONLY)
> [L27] - TRANSUNION SUMMARY (OPEN + CLOSED)
> [L28] 
> [L29] Required keys (appear exactly once; blank value allowed):
> [L30] Report Date
> [L31] Bureau
> [L32] FICO Score 8
> [L33] 
> [L34] Counts:
> [L35] Total Open Accounts
> [L36] Total Closed Accounts
> [L37] Total Accounts
> [L38] Accounts Included (This Output)
> [L39] 
> [L40] Ages:
> [L41] Average Account Age (yrs decimal)   (4dp)
> [L42] Average Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L43] Oldest Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L44] Newest Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L45] 
> [L46] Debt totals (numeric or blank):
> [L47] Grand Total Debt
> [L48] Total Revolving Debt
> [L49] Total Auto Loan Debt
> [L50] Total Personal Loan Debt
> [L51] Total Student Loan Debt
> [L52] Total Mortgage Debt
> [L53] Total Other Debt
> [L54] 
> [L55] Revolving aggregates:
> [L56] Total Revolving Credit Limits
> [L57] Overall Revolving Utilization %
> [L58] 
> [L59] Tier counts (ONLY for REVOLVING; ordered low-risk → high-risk):
> [L60] Tier Count — Excellent (0%)
> [L61] Tier Count — Excellent (1–3%)
> [L62] Tier Count — Low (4–7%)
> [L63] Tier Count — Review (8–9%)
> [L64] Tier Count — Medium (10%+)
> [L65] Tier Count — Medium-High (20%+)
> [L66] Tier Count — High (30%+)
> [L67] 
> [L68] Loan flags:
> [L69] Loan Remaining ≥85% Count
> [L70] 
> [L71] Explicit exclusions (MUST NOT appear):
> [L72] DTI
> [L73] Strategic Notes
> [L74] Total Account Age (yrs)
> [L75] Total Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L76] 
> [L77] ====================
> [L78] C) TSV SCHEMA (ORDERED, EXACT)
> [L79] ====================
> [L80] Bureau\tCreditor Name\tOpening Date\tAge (yrs decimal)\tCredit Limit\tBalance\tUtilization %\tUtilization Tier\tHighest Balance\tOriginal Balance\tAccount Type\tTradeline Type\tResponsibility\tStatus\tStatus Updated Date\tOpen/Closed\tUnderwriting Notes\tAdditional Notes\tFlags
> [L81] 
> [L82] Rules:
> [L83] - Age (yrs+mos) MUST NOT be a TSV column.
> [L84] - Underwriting Notes / Additional Notes blank unless explicitly present.
> [L85] 
> [L86] ====================
> [L87] D) AGE (yrs decimal) — REQUIRED WHEN POSSIBLE
> [L88] ====================
> [L89] - As-of date = report_date
> [L90] - Compute elapsed FULL MONTHS between Opening Date and report_date.
> [L91] - Age (yrs decimal) = months/12 rounded to EXACTLY 4 decimals.
> [L92] - If missing/unparseable dates => Age blank; add AGE_NOT_COMPUTED.
> [L93] 
> [L94] ====================
> [L95] E) UTILIZATION — REVOLVING ONLY (STRICT)
> [L96] ====================
> [L97] - Compute Utilization % and Tier ONLY if Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L98] - For DEPOSIT/OTHER (including overdraft/reserve checking): utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L99] - For INSTALLMENT/LOAN: utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L100] 
> [L101] Utilization %:
> [L102] Balance / Credit Limit * 100, round 2 decimals.

> Content source: Source.file
> Title: MASTER_INDEX.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn1file12
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] MASTER INDEX — Credit Report TSV Extractor
> [L2] Spec Version: v2.2_FAILSAFE_INDEX_NAMES
> [L3] 
> [L4] MISSION
> [L5] Produce contract-compliant outputs:
> [L6] 
> [L7] OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L8] - Summary (2 columns, tab-separated)
> [L9] - TSV (tab-separated; exact header)
> [L10] 
> [L11] OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L12] - Summary (2 columns, tab-separated)
> [L13] - TSV (tab-separated; exact header)
> [L14] 
> [L15] FILE PRECEDENCE (HIGHEST→LOWEST)
> [L16] 1) SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS.txt
> [L17] 2) MASTER_INDEX.txt (or MASTER_INDEX.txt)
> [L18] 3) SPEC_CORE.txt
> [L19] 4) QA_AND_GATES.txt
> [L20] 5) COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST.txt
> [L21] 
> [L22] EXECUTION FLOW
> [L23] 1) Read MASTER_INDEX.
> [L24] 2) Parse metadata: bureau, report_date (as-of), fico if present.
> [L25] 3) Parse tradelines: ACCOUNT rows only; never mix INQUIRY into tradeline TSV.
> [L26] 4) Derive fields; split Output A/B; sort; build summaries.
> [L27] 5) Run CONTRACT OUTPUT GATE. If fail => QA FAIL only.

> [L17] About your FICO Score 8 or other FICO Scores
> [L18] Your FICO Score 8 powered by Experian data is formulated using the information in your credit ,le at the time it is requested. Many but not all
> [L19] lenders use FICO Score 8. In addition to the FICO Score 8, we may offer and provide other base or industry-speci,c FICO Scores (such as
> [L20] FICO Auto Scores and FICO Bankcard Scores). The other FICO Scores made available are calculated from versions of the base and
> [L21] industry-speci,c FICO Score models.
> [L22] Base FICO Scores (including the FICO Score 8) range from 300 to 850. Industry-speci,c FICO Scores range from 250-900. Higher scores
> [L23] represent a greater likelihood that you'll pay back your debts so you are viewed as being a lower credit risk to lenders. A lower FICO Score
> [L24] indicates to lenders that you may be a higher credit risk. There are many scoring models used in the marketplace. The type of score used, and
> [L25] its associated risk levels, may vary from lender to lender. But regardless of what scoring model is used, they all have one purpose: to
> [L26] summarize your creditworthiness. Keep in mind that your score is just one factor used in the application process. Other factors, such as your
> [L27] annual salary and length of employment, may also be considered by lenders when you apply for a loan.
> [L28] What this means to you:
> [L29] Credit scoring can help you understand your overall credit rating and help companies better understand how to serve you. Overall bene,ts of
> [L30] credit scoring have included faster credit approvals, reduction in human error and bias, consistency, and better terms and rates for American
> [L31] consumers through reduced costs and losses for lenders. Your lender or insurer may use a different FICO Score than FICO Score 8 or other
> [L32] base or industry-speci,c FICO Scores provided by us, or different scoring models to determine how you score.

> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn2file1
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] SPEC CORE — v2.3 (Contract + Audit-Friendly)
> [L2] 
> [L3] ====================
> [L4] A) OUTPUT BLOCK FORMAT (EXACT)
> [L5] ====================
> [L6] OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L7] <Summary A: 2 columns, TAB-separated; one key per line>
> [L8] <blank line>
> [L9] <TSV A: header row + rows, TAB-separated>
> [L10] 
> [L11] <blank line>
> [L12] OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L13] <Summary B: 2 columns, TAB-separated; one key per line>
> [L14] <blank line>
> [L15] <TSV B: header row + rows, TAB-separated>
> [L16] 
> [L17] No other text is allowed.
> [L18] 
> [L19] ====================
> [L20] B) SUMMARY (2 COLUMNS, TAB-SEPARATED; REQUIRED KEYS)
> [L21] ====================
> [L22] - Each summary line MUST be: <Key>\t<Value>
> [L23] - No header row like "Summary Key Value" (forbidden).
> [L24] 
> [L25] Bureau label (TU):
> [L26] - TRANSUNION SUMMARY (OPEN ONLY)
> [L27] - TRANSUNION SUMMARY (OPEN + CLOSED)
> [L28] 
> [L29] Required keys (appear exactly once; blank value allowed):
> [L30] Report Date
> [L31] Bureau
> [L32] FICO Score 8
> [L33] 
> [L34] Counts:
> [L35] Total Open Accounts
> [L36] Total Closed Accounts
> [L37] Total Accounts
> [L38] Accounts Included (This Output)
> [L39] 
> [L40] Ages:
> [L41] Average Account Age (yrs decimal)   (4dp)
> [L42] Average Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L43] Oldest Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L44] Newest Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L45] 
> [L46] Debt totals (numeric or blank):
> [L47] Grand Total Debt
> [L48] Total Revolving Debt
> [L49] Total Auto Loan Debt
> [L50] Total Personal Loan Debt
> [L51] Total Student Loan Debt
> [L52] Total Mortgage Debt
> [L53] Total Other Debt
> [L54] 
> [L55] Revolving aggregates:
> [L56] Total Revolving Credit Limits
> [L57] Overall Revolving Utilization %
> [L58] 
> [L59] Tier counts (ONLY for REVOLVING; ordered low-risk → high-risk):
> [L60] Tier Count — Excellent (0%)
> [L61] Tier Count — Excellent (1–3%)
> [L62] Tier Count — Low (4–7%)
> [L63] Tier Count — Review (8–9%)
> [L64] Tier Count — Medium (10%+)
> [L65] Tier Count — Medium-High (20%+)
> [L66] Tier Count — High (30%+)
> [L67] 
> [L68] Loan flags:
> [L69] Loan Remaining ≥85% Count
> [L70] 
> [L71] Explicit exclusions (MUST NOT appear):
> [L72] DTI
> [L73] Strategic Notes
> [L74] Total Account Age (yrs)
> [L75] Total Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L76] 
> [L77] ====================
> [L78] C) TSV SCHEMA (ORDERED, EXACT)
> [L79] ====================
> [L80] Bureau\tCreditor Name\tOpening Date\tAge (yrs decimal)\tCredit Limit\tBalance\tUtilization %\tUtilization Tier\tHighest Balance\tOriginal Balance\tAccount Type\tTradeline Type\tResponsibility\tStatus\tStatus Updated Date\tOpen/Closed\tUnderwriting Notes\tAdditional Notes\tFlags
> [L81] 
> [L82] Rules:
> [L83] - Age (yrs+mos) MUST NOT be a TSV column.
> [L84] - Underwriting Notes / Additional Notes blank unless explicitly present.
> [L85] 
> [L86] ====================
> [L87] D) AGE (yrs decimal) — REQUIRED WHEN POSSIBLE
> [L88] ====================
> [L89] - As-of date = report_date
> [L90] - Compute elapsed FULL MONTHS between Opening Date and report_date.
> [L91] - Age (yrs decimal) = months/12 rounded to EXACTLY 4 decimals.
> [L92] - If missing/unparseable dates => Age blank; add AGE_NOT_COMPUTED.
> [L93] 
> [L94] ====================
> [L95] E) UTILIZATION — REVOLVING ONLY (STRICT)
> [L96] ====================
> [L97] - Compute Utilization % and Tier ONLY if Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L98] - For DEPOSIT/OTHER (including overdraft/reserve checking): utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L99] - For INSTALLMENT/LOAN: utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L100] 
> [L101] Utilization %:
> [L102] Balance / Credit Limit * 100, round 2 decimals.

> [L8] Total Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L9] 
> [L10] ====================
> [L11] C) TSV SCHEMA (ORDERED, EXACT)
> [L12] ====================
> [L13] Bureau\tCreditor Name\tOpening Date\tAge (yrs decimal)\tCredit Limit\tBalance\tUtilization %\tUtilization Tier\tHighest Balance\tOriginal Balance\tAccount Type\tTradeline Type\tResponsibility\tStatus\tStatus Updated Date\tOpen/Closed\tUnderwriting Notes\tAdditional Notes\tFlags
> [L14] 
> [L15] Rules:
> [L16] - Age (yrs+mos) MUST NOT be a TSV column.
> [L17] - Underwriting Notes / Additional Notes blank unless explicitly present.
> [L18] 
> [L19] ====================
> [L20] D) AGE (yrs decimal) — REQUIRED WHEN POSSIBLE
> [L21] ====================
> [L22] - As-of date = report_date
> [L23] - Compute elapsed FULL MONTHS between Opening Date and report_date.
> [L24] - Age (yrs decimal) = months/12 rounded to EXACTLY 4 decimals.
> [L25] - If missing/unparseable dates => Age blank; add AGE_NOT_COMPUTED.
> [L26] 
> [L27] ====================
> [L28] E) UTILIZATION — REVOLVING ONLY (STRICT)
> [L29] ====================
> [L30] - Compute Utilization % and Tier ONLY if Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L31] - For DEPOSIT/OTHER (including overdraft/reserve checking): utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L32] - For INSTALLMENT/LOAN: utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L33] 
> [L34] Utilization %:
> [L35] Balance / Credit Limit * 100, round 2 decimals.
> [L36] 
> [L37] Allowed tier labels (EXACT; no numeric ranges like "1–9%" allowed):
> [L38] Excellent (0%)
> [L39] Excellent (1–3%)
> [L40] Low (4–7%)
> [L41] Review (8–9%)
> [L42] Medium (10%+)
> [L43] Medium-High (20%+)
> [L44] High (30%+)
> [L45] 
> [L46] ====================
> [L47] F) ACCOUNT TYPE NORMALIZATION
> [L48] ====================
> [L49] REVOLVING: credit cards, bankcards, LOC.
> [L50] INSTALLMENT/LOAN: student/auto/personal/mortgage loans.
> [L51] DEPOSIT/OTHER: overdraft / reserve checking accounts and similar.
> [L52] 
> [L53] If unclear: blank + AMBIGUOUS_ACCOUNT_TYPE
> [L54] 
> [L55] ====================
> [L56] G) LOAN REMAINING FLAG (LOANS ONLY)
> [L57] ====================
> [L58] Remaining % = Balance / Original Balance (only if Original Balance present and >0)
> [L59] If Remaining % >= 85%: add LOAN_HIGH_REMAINING_85PCT
> [L60] Never apply to REVOLVING or DEPOSIT/OTHER.
> [L61] 
> [L62] ====================
> [L63] H) SORTING (REQUIRED)
> [L64] ====================
> [L65] Sort by Opening Date oldest→newest for parseable dates.
> [L66] Missing/unparseable dates go last (stable) and add OPEN_DATE_UNPARSEABLE.

> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn2file4
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] SPEC CORE — v2.2 (Contract)
> [L2] 
> [L3] TSV HEADER (EXACT)
> [L4] Bureau	Creditor Name	Opening Date	Age (yrs decimal)	Credit Limit	Balance	Utilization %	Utilization Tier	Highest Balance	Original Balance	Account Type	Tradeline Type	Responsibility	Status	Status Updated Date	Open/Closed	Underwriting Notes	Additional Notes	Flags
> [L5] 
> [L6] RULES (KEY)
> [L7] - Age (yrs+mos) is SUMMARY-ONLY.
> [L8] - Age (yrs decimal) = full months / 12, EXACTLY 4 decimals, as-of report_date.
> [L9] - Sort by Opening Date oldest→newest; missing/unparseable last (stable).
> [L10] - Utilization + tiers ONLY for REVOLVING; loans must have util blanks.
> [L11] - Loan Remaining >=85% (Balance/Original Balance) => LOAN_HIGH_REMAINING_85PCT (loans only).
> [L12] - Do NOT dedupe; flag DUPLICATE_REVIEW if suspected.

> Title: MASTER_INDEX.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn2file6
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] MASTER INDEX — Credit Report TSV Extractor
> [L2] Spec Version: v2.3_CONTRACT_DEBUGGABLE
> [L3] Checklist Authority: MASTER_CHECKLIST_FINAL_v3
> [L4] 
> [L5] MISSION
> [L6] Produce contract-compliant, Google-Sheets-ready outputs:
> [L7] 
> [L8] OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L9] - Summary (2 columns, TAB-separated)
> [L10] - TSV (TAB-separated; exact header)
> [L11] 
> [L12] OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L13] - Summary (2 columns, TAB-separated)
> [L14] - TSV (TAB-separated; exact header)
> [L15] 
> [L16] FILE PRECEDENCE (HIGHEST→LOWEST)
> [L17] 1) SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS.txt
> [L18] 2) MASTER_INDEX.txt
> [L19] 3) SPEC_CORE.txt
> [L20] 4) QA_AND_GATES.txt
> [L21] 5) COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST.txt
> [L22] 
> [L23] EXECUTION FLOW (DETERMINISTIC)
> [L24] 1) Extract metadata:
> [L25]    - bureau
> [L26]    - report_date (as-of date) REQUIRED for age calculations
> [L27]    - fico score if present (summary only)
> [L28] 2) Parse tradelines (ACCOUNT rows only). Never include INQUIRY in tradeline TSV.
> [L29] 3) Normalize Account Type:
> [L30]    - REVOLVING (credit card/LOC)
> [L31]    - INSTALLMENT/LOAN
> [L32]    - DEPOSIT/OTHER (e.g., overdraft/reserve checking)
> [L33] 4) Compute derived fields per SPEC_CORE.
> [L34] 5) Build Output A (Open only) and Output B (Open+Closed).
> [L35] 6) Sort within each output by Opening Date oldest→newest; missing/unparseable last (stable).
> [L36] 7) Build Summary blocks (2 columns, tab-separated) per SPEC_CORE required keys.
> [L37] 8) Run QA gates (including reconciliation gates). If any fail => QA FAIL with details + fix hints.
> [L38] 9) Output:
> [L39]    - PASS => Output A block then Output B block (no extra text)
> [L40]    - FAIL => QA FAIL block only
> # 【7†QA_AND_GATES.txt†file-LsNs8c2SDJcfvZhQBB8JUX】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: QA_AND_GATES.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn2file7
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] QA AND GATES — v2.2 (Contract Gate)
> [L2] 
> [L3] HARD FAIL (FAIL FAST)
> [L4] - unreadable_page
> [L5] - fragmented_row
> [L6] 
> [L7] CONTRACT OUTPUT GATE (HARD FAIL)
> [L8] Fail if ANY are true:
> [L9] - Missing Output A or Output B block
> [L10] - Missing Summary or TSV in either block
> [L11] - TSV header mismatch (must match SPEC_CORE exactly)
> [L12] - Any INQUIRY rows appear inside TSV tables
> [L13] - Age (yrs+mos) appears as TSV column
> [L14] - Any non-blank Age (yrs decimal) not exactly 4 decimals
> [L15] - Any utilization tier on non-REVOLVING rows
> [L16] - Any text outside contract blocks (narration leakage)
> [L17] 
> [L18] QA FAIL FORMAT (ONLY OUTPUT ON FAIL)
> [L19] QA FAIL — <STAGE>
> [L20] Reason	Details
> [L21] <reason>	<details>
> # 【8†QA_AND_GATES.txt†file-CrkACVyC7oc2ktmtAoMZh5】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: QA_AND_GATES.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn2file8
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] QA AND GATES — v2.3 (HARD FAIL ONLY + CONTRACT GATE + RECONCILIATION)
> [L2] 
> [L3] HARD FAIL (FAIL FAST)
> [L4] - unreadable_page
> [L5] - fragmented_row
> [L6] 
> [L7] CONTRACT OUTPUT GATE (HARD FAIL)
> [L8] Fail if ANY are true:
> [L9] (1) Missing either block header:
> [L10]     OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L11]     OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L12] (2) Missing Summary or TSV in either block.
> [L13] (3) TSV header mismatch (must match SPEC_CORE exactly).
> [L14] (4) Summary is not 2-column TAB-separated (e.g., contains "Summary Key Value").
> [L15] (5) Any INQUIRY rows appear inside TSV tables.
> [L16] (6) Any text outside contract blocks (narration leakage).
> [L17] 
> [L18] FIELD FORMAT GATES (HARD FAIL)
> [L19] (7) Any non-blank Age (yrs decimal) is not exactly 4 decimals.
> [L20] (8) Any utilization tier appears on non-REVOLVING rows.
> [L21] (9) Any utilization tier label not in the allowed set.
> [L22] (10) Any DEPOSIT/OTHER row has utilization % or tier non-blank.
> [L23] 
> [L24] RECONCILIATION GATES (HARD FAIL; DEBUGGABLE)
> [L25] Definitions:
> [L26] - A_row_count = number of TSV rows in Output A.
> [L27] - B_row_count = number of TSV rows in Output B.
> [L28] - A_open_count_from_rows = count(Open/Closed == Open) in Output A.
> [L29] - B_open_count_from_rows / B_closed_count_from_rows similarly.
> [L30] - Debt totals computed from TSV rows (numeric parse; blanks ignored).
> [L31] 
> [L32] Fail if ANY are true:
> [L33] (11) Summary key "Accounts Included (This Output)" != row_count for that output.
> [L34] (12) For Output A: Summary "Total Open Accounts" != A_row_count.
> [L35] (13) For Output B: Summary "Total Accounts" != B_row_count.
> [L36] (14) For Output B: Summary "Total Open Accounts" != B_open_count_from_rows.
> [L37] (15) For Output B: Summary "Total Closed Accounts" != B_closed_count_from_rows.
> [L38] (16) Summary "Grand Total Debt" != sum of balances across rows (by numeric parsing).
> [L39] (17) Summary "Total Revolving Debt" != sum of balances where Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L40] (18) Tier counts sum != number of REVOLVING rows with computable utilization.
> [L41] 
> [L42] QA FAIL OUTPUT (ONLY OUTPUT ON FAIL)
> [L43] Format:
> [L44] QA FAIL — <STAGE>
> [L45] Reason\tDetails
> [L46] <reason>\t<details>
> [L47] 
> [L48] DEBUG DETAIL REQUIREMENT
> [L49] - Details MUST include: gate_id, expected, observed, and a fix_hint.
> [L50] - If a row triggers a gate, include a row identifier: "<Creditor Name>|<Opening Date>|<Open/Closed>".

> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn3file0
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] Loan flags:
> [L2] Loan Remaining ≥85% Count
> [L3] 
> [L4] Explicit exclusions (MUST NOT appear):
> [L5] DTI
> [L6] Strategic Notes
> [L7] Total Account Age (yrs)
> [L8] Total Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L9] 
> [L10] ====================
> [L11] C) TSV SCHEMA (ORDERED, EXACT)
> [L12] ====================
> [L13] Bureau\tCreditor Name\tOpening Date\tAge (yrs decimal)\tCredit Limit\tBalance\tUtilization %\tUtilization Tier\tHighest Balance\tOriginal Balance\tAccount Type\tTradeline Type\tResponsibility\tStatus\tStatus Updated Date\tOpen/Closed\tUnderwriting Notes\tAdditional Notes\tFlags
> [L14] 
> [L15] Rules:
> [L16] - Age (yrs+mos) MUST NOT be a TSV column.
> [L17] - Underwriting Notes / Additional Notes blank unless explicitly present.
> [L18] 
> [L19] ====================
> [L20] D) AGE (yrs decimal) — REQUIRED WHEN POSSIBLE
> [L21] ====================
> [L22] - As-of date = report_date
> [L23] - Compute elapsed FULL MONTHS between Opening Date and report_date.
> [L24] - Age (yrs decimal) = months/12 rounded to EXACTLY 4 decimals.
> [L25] - If missing/unparseable dates => Age blank; add AGE_NOT_COMPUTED.
> [L26] 
> [L27] ====================
> [L28] E) UTILIZATION — REVOLVING ONLY (STRICT)
> [L29] ====================
> [L30] - Compute Utilization % and Tier ONLY if Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L31] - For DEPOSIT/OTHER (including overdraft/reserve checking): utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L32] - For INSTALLMENT/LOAN: utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L33] 
> [L34] Utilization %:
> [L35] Balance / Credit Limit * 100, round 2 decimals.
> [L36] 
> [L37] Allowed tier labels (EXACT; no numeric ranges like "1–9%" allowed):
> [L38] Excellent (0%)
> [L39] Excellent (1–3%)
> [L40] Low (4–7%)
> [L41] Review (8–9%)
> [L42] Medium (10%+)
> [L43] Medium-High (20%+)
> [L44] High (30%+)
> [L45] 
> [L46] ====================
> [L47] F) ACCOUNT TYPE NORMALIZATION
> [L48] ====================
> [L49] REVOLVING: credit cards, bankcards, LOC.
> [L50] INSTALLMENT/LOAN: student/auto/personal/mortgage loans.
> [L51] DEPOSIT/OTHER: overdraft / reserve checking accounts and similar.
> [L52] 
> [L53] If unclear: blank + AMBIGUOUS_ACCOUNT_TYPE
> [L54] 
> [L55] ====================
> [L56] G) LOAN REMAINING FLAG (LOANS ONLY)
> [L57] ====================
> [L58] Remaining % = Balance / Original Balance (only if Original Balance present and >0)
> [L59] If Remaining % >= 85%: add LOAN_HIGH_REMAINING_85PCT
> [L60] Never apply to REVOLVING or DEPOSIT/OTHER.
> [L61] 
> [L62] ====================
> [L63] H) SORTING (REQUIRED)
> [L64] ====================
> [L65] Sort by Opening Date oldest→newest for parseable dates.
> [L66] Missing/unparseable dates go last (stable) and add OPEN_DATE_UNPARSEABLE.

> [L102] Balance / Credit Limit * 100, round 2 decimals.
> # 【2†SPEC_CORE.txt†file-RYWNxz9NqmVATC8kcBrYYv】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn3file2
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] SPEC CORE — v2.2 (Contract)
> [L2] 
> [L3] TSV HEADER (EXACT)
> [L4] Bureau	Creditor Name	Opening Date	Age (yrs decimal)	Credit Limit	Balance	Utilization %	Utilization Tier	Highest Balance	Original Balance	Account Type	Tradeline Type	Responsibility	Status	Status Updated Date	Open/Closed	Underwriting Notes	Additional Notes	Flags
> [L5] 
> [L6] RULES (KEY)
> [L7] - Age (yrs+mos) is SUMMARY-ONLY.
> [L8] - Age (yrs decimal) = full months / 12, EXACTLY 4 decimals, as-of report_date.
> [L9] - Sort by Opening Date oldest→newest; missing/unparseable last (stable).
> [L10] - Utilization + tiers ONLY for REVOLVING; loans must have util blanks.
> [L11] - Loan Remaining >=85% (Balance/Original Balance) => LOAN_HIGH_REMAINING_85PCT (loans only).
> [L12] - Do NOT dedupe; flag DUPLICATE_REVIEW if suspected.

> # 【5†MASTER_INDEX.txt†file-889aKgioqc2WDHFBCLf6ZU】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: MASTER_INDEX.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn3file5
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] MASTER INDEX — Credit Report TSV Extractor
> [L2] Spec Version: v2.3_CONTRACT_DEBUGGABLE
> [L3] Checklist Authority: MASTER_CHECKLIST_FINAL_v3
> [L4] 
> [L5] MISSION
> [L6] Produce contract-compliant, Google-Sheets-ready outputs:
> [L7] 
> [L8] OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L9] - Summary (2 columns, TAB-separated)
> [L10] - TSV (TAB-separated; exact header)
> [L11] 
> [L12] OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L13] - Summary (2 columns, TAB-separated)
> [L14] - TSV (TAB-separated; exact header)
> [L15] 
> [L16] FILE PRECEDENCE (HIGHEST→LOWEST)
> [L17] 1) SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS.txt
> [L18] 2) MASTER_INDEX.txt
> [L19] 3) SPEC_CORE.txt
> [L20] 4) QA_AND_GATES.txt
> [L21] 5) COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST.txt
> [L22] 
> [L23] EXECUTION FLOW (DETERMINISTIC)
> [L24] 1) Extract metadata:
> [L25]    - bureau
> [L26]    - report_date (as-of date) REQUIRED for age calculations
> [L27]    - fico score if present (summary only)
> [L28] 2) Parse tradelines (ACCOUNT rows only). Never include INQUIRY in tradeline TSV.
> [L29] 3) Normalize Account Type:
> [L30]    - REVOLVING (credit card/LOC)
> [L31]    - INSTALLMENT/LOAN
> [L32]    - DEPOSIT/OTHER (e.g., overdraft/reserve checking)
> [L33] 4) Compute derived fields per SPEC_CORE.
> [L34] 5) Build Output A (Open only) and Output B (Open+Closed).
> [L35] 6) Sort within each output by Opening Date oldest→newest; missing/unparseable last (stable).
> [L36] 7) Build Summary blocks (2 columns, tab-separated) per SPEC_CORE required keys.
> [L37] 8) Run QA gates (including reconciliation gates). If any fail => QA FAIL with details + fix hints.
> [L38] 9) Output:
> [L39]    - PASS => Output A block then Output B block (no extra text)
> [L40]    - FAIL => QA FAIL block only
> # 【6†QA_AND_GATES.txt†file-LsNs8c2SDJcfvZhQBB8JUX】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: QA_AND_GATES.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn3file6
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] QA AND GATES — v2.2 (Contract Gate)
> [L2] 
> [L3] HARD FAIL (FAIL FAST)
> [L4] - unreadable_page
> [L5] - fragmented_row
> [L6] 
> [L7] CONTRACT OUTPUT GATE (HARD FAIL)
> [L8] Fail if ANY are true:
> [L9] - Missing Output A or Output B block
> [L10] - Missing Summary or TSV in either block
> [L11] - TSV header mismatch (must match SPEC_CORE exactly)
> [L12] - Any INQUIRY rows appear inside TSV tables
> [L13] - Age (yrs+mos) appears as TSV column
> [L14] - Any non-blank Age (yrs decimal) not exactly 4 decimals
> [L15] - Any utilization tier on non-REVOLVING rows
> [L16] - Any text outside contract blocks (narration leakage)
> [L17] 
> [L18] QA FAIL FORMAT (ONLY OUTPUT ON FAIL)
> [L19] QA FAIL — <STAGE>
> [L20] Reason	Details
> [L21] <reason>	<details>
> # 【7†QA_AND_GATES.txt†file-CrkACVyC7oc2ktmtAoMZh5】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: QA_AND_GATES.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn3file7
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] QA AND GATES — v2.3 (HARD FAIL ONLY + CONTRACT GATE + RECONCILIATION)
> [L2] 
> [L3] HARD FAIL (FAIL FAST)
> [L4] - unreadable_page
> [L5] - fragmented_row
> [L6] 
> [L7] CONTRACT OUTPUT GATE (HARD FAIL)
> [L8] Fail if ANY are true:
> [L9] (1) Missing either block header:
> [L10]     OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L11]     OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L12] (2) Missing Summary or TSV in either block.
> [L13] (3) TSV header mismatch (must match SPEC_CORE exactly).
> [L14] (4) Summary is not 2-column TAB-separated (e.g., contains "Summary Key Value").
> [L15] (5) Any INQUIRY rows appear inside TSV tables.
> [L16] (6) Any text outside contract blocks (narration leakage).
> [L17] 
> [L18] FIELD FORMAT GATES (HARD FAIL)
> [L19] (7) Any non-blank Age (yrs decimal) is not exactly 4 decimals.
> [L20] (8) Any utilization tier appears on non-REVOLVING rows.
> [L21] (9) Any utilization tier label not in the allowed set.
> [L22] (10) Any DEPOSIT/OTHER row has utilization % or tier non-blank.
> [L23] 
> [L24] RECONCILIATION GATES (HARD FAIL; DEBUGGABLE)
> [L25] Definitions:
> [L26] - A_row_count = number of TSV rows in Output A.
> [L27] - B_row_count = number of TSV rows in Output B.
> [L28] - A_open_count_from_rows = count(Open/Closed == Open) in Output A.
> [L29] - B_open_count_from_rows / B_closed_count_from_rows similarly.
> [L30] - Debt totals computed from TSV rows (numeric parse; blanks ignored).
> [L31] 
> [L32] Fail if ANY are true:
> [L33] (11) Summary key "Accounts Included (This Output)" != row_count for that output.
> [L34] (12) For Output A: Summary "Total Open Accounts" != A_row_count.
> [L35] (13) For Output B: Summary "Total Accounts" != B_row_count.
> [L36] (14) For Output B: Summary "Total Open Accounts" != B_open_count_from_rows.
> [L37] (15) For Output B: Summary "Total Closed Accounts" != B_closed_count_from_rows.
> [L38] (16) Summary "Grand Total Debt" != sum of balances across rows (by numeric parsing).
> [L39] (17) Summary "Total Revolving Debt" != sum of balances where Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L40] (18) Tier counts sum != number of REVOLVING rows with computable utilization.
> [L41] 
> [L42] QA FAIL OUTPUT (ONLY OUTPUT ON FAIL)
> [L43] Format:
> [L44] QA FAIL — <STAGE>
> [L45] Reason\tDetails
> [L46] <reason>\t<details>
> [L47] 
> [L48] DEBUG DETAIL REQUIREMENT
> [L49] - Details MUST include: gate_id, expected, observed, and a fix_hint.
> [L50] - If a row triggers a gate, include a row identifier: "<Creditor Name>|<Opening Date>|<Open/Closed>".

> # 【2†SPEC_CORE.txt†file-RYWNxz9NqmVATC8kcBrYYv】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn4file2
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] SPEC CORE — v2.2 (Contract)
> [L2] 
> [L3] TSV HEADER (EXACT)
> [L4] Bureau	Creditor Name	Opening Date	Age (yrs decimal)	Credit Limit	Balance	Utilization %	Utilization Tier	Highest Balance	Original Balance	Account Type	Tradeline Type	Responsibility	Status	Status Updated Date	Open/Closed	Underwriting Notes	Additional Notes	Flags
> [L5] 
> [L6] RULES (KEY)
> [L7] - Age (yrs+mos) is SUMMARY-ONLY.
> [L8] - Age (yrs decimal) = full months / 12, EXACTLY 4 decimals, as-of report_date.
> [L9] - Sort by Opening Date oldest→newest; missing/unparseable last (stable).
> [L10] - Utilization + tiers ONLY for REVOLVING; loans must have util blanks.
> [L11] - Loan Remaining >=85% (Balance/Original Balance) => LOAN_HIGH_REMAINING_85PCT (loans only).
> [L12] - Do NOT dedupe; flag DUPLICATE_REVIEW if suspected.
> # 【3†MASTER_INDEX.txt†file-Y3JytvPntPTm3JMbKGuFC3】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: MASTER_INDEX.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn4file3
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] MASTER INDEX — Credit Report TSV Extractor
> [L2] Spec Version: v2.2_FAILSAFE_INDEX_NAMES
> [L3] 
> [L4] MISSION
> [L5] Produce contract-compliant outputs:
> [L6] 
> [L7] OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L8] - Summary (2 columns, tab-separated)
> [L9] - TSV (tab-separated; exact header)
> [L10] 
> [L11] OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L12] - Summary (2 columns, tab-separated)
> [L13] - TSV (tab-separated; exact header)
> [L14] 
> [L15] FILE PRECEDENCE (HIGHEST→LOWEST)
> [L16] 1) SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS.txt
> [L17] 2) MASTER_INDEX.txt (or MASTER_INDEX.txt)
> [L18] 3) SPEC_CORE.txt
> [L19] 4) QA_AND_GATES.txt
> [L20] 5) COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST.txt
> [L21] 
> [L22] EXECUTION FLOW
> [L23] 1) Read MASTER_INDEX.
> [L24] 2) Parse metadata: bureau, report_date (as-of), fico if present.
> [L25] 3) Parse tradelines: ACCOUNT rows only; never mix INQUIRY into tradeline TSV.
> [L26] 4) Derive fields; split Output A/B; sort; build summaries.
> [L27] 5) Run CONTRACT OUTPUT GATE. If fail => QA FAIL only.
> # 【4†MASTER_INDEX.txt†file-889aKgioqc2WDHFBCLf6ZU】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: MASTER_INDEX.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn4file4
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] MASTER INDEX — Credit Report TSV Extractor
> [L2] Spec Version: v2.3_CONTRACT_DEBUGGABLE
> [L3] Checklist Authority: MASTER_CHECKLIST_FINAL_v3
> [L4] 
> [L5] MISSION
> [L6] Produce contract-compliant, Google-Sheets-ready outputs:
> [L7] 
> [L8] OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L9] - Summary (2 columns, TAB-separated)
> [L10] - TSV (TAB-separated; exact header)
> [L11] 
> [L12] OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L13] - Summary (2 columns, TAB-separated)
> [L14] - TSV (TAB-separated; exact header)
> [L15] 
> [L16] FILE PRECEDENCE (HIGHEST→LOWEST)
> [L17] 1) SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS.txt
> [L18] 2) MASTER_INDEX.txt
> [L19] 3) SPEC_CORE.txt
> [L20] 4) QA_AND_GATES.txt
> [L21] 5) COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST.txt
> [L22] 
> [L23] EXECUTION FLOW (DETERMINISTIC)
> [L24] 1) Extract metadata:
> [L25]    - bureau
> [L26]    - report_date (as-of date) REQUIRED for age calculations
> [L27]    - fico score if present (summary only)
> [L28] 2) Parse tradelines (ACCOUNT rows only). Never include INQUIRY in tradeline TSV.
> [L29] 3) Normalize Account Type:
> [L30]    - REVOLVING (credit card/LOC)
> [L31]    - INSTALLMENT/LOAN
> [L32]    - DEPOSIT/OTHER (e.g., overdraft/reserve checking)
> [L33] 4) Compute derived fields per SPEC_CORE.
> [L34] 5) Build Output A (Open only) and Output B (Open+Closed).
> [L35] 6) Sort within each output by Opening Date oldest→newest; missing/unparseable last (stable).
> [L36] 7) Build Summary blocks (2 columns, tab-separated) per SPEC_CORE required keys.
> [L37] 8) Run QA gates (including reconciliation gates). If any fail => QA FAIL with details + fix hints.
> [L38] 9) Output:
> [L39]    - PASS => Output A block then Output B block (no extra text)
> [L40]    - FAIL => QA FAIL block only

> # 【6†SPEC_CORE.txt†file-UdH7PZmV8pCLCqJ554PU6J】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn4file6
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] SPEC CORE — v2.3 (Contract + Audit-Friendly)
> [L2] 
> [L3] ====================
> [L4] A) OUTPUT BLOCK FORMAT (EXACT)
> [L5] ====================
> [L6] OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L7] <Summary A: 2 columns, TAB-separated; one key per line>
> [L8] <blank line>
> [L9] <TSV A: header row + rows, TAB-separated>
> [L10] 
> [L11] <blank line>
> [L12] OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L13] <Summary B: 2 columns, TAB-separated; one key per line>
> [L14] <blank line>
> [L15] <TSV B: header row + rows, TAB-separated>
> [L16] 
> [L17] No other text is allowed.
> [L18] 
> [L19] ====================
> [L20] B) SUMMARY (2 COLUMNS, TAB-SEPARATED; REQUIRED KEYS)
> [L21] ====================
> [L22] - Each summary line MUST be: <Key>\t<Value>
> [L23] - No header row like "Summary Key Value" (forbidden).
> [L24] 
> [L25] Bureau label (TU):
> [L26] - TRANSUNION SUMMARY (OPEN ONLY)
> [L27] - TRANSUNION SUMMARY (OPEN + CLOSED)
> [L28] 
> [L29] Required keys (appear exactly once; blank value allowed):
> [L30] Report Date
> [L31] Bureau
> [L32] FICO Score 8
> [L33] 
> [L34] Counts:
> [L35] Total Open Accounts
> [L36] Total Closed Accounts
> [L37] Total Accounts
> [L38] Accounts Included (This Output)
> [L39] 
> [L40] Ages:
> [L41] Average Account Age (yrs decimal)   (4dp)
> [L42] Average Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L43] Oldest Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L44] Newest Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L45] 
> [L46] Debt totals (numeric or blank):
> [L47] Grand Total Debt
> [L48] Total Revolving Debt
> [L49] Total Auto Loan Debt
> [L50] Total Personal Loan Debt
> [L51] Total Student Loan Debt
> [L52] Total Mortgage Debt
> [L53] Total Other Debt
> [L54] 
> [L55] Revolving aggregates:
> [L56] Total Revolving Credit Limits
> [L57] Overall Revolving Utilization %
> [L58] 
> [L59] Tier counts (ONLY for REVOLVING; ordered low-risk → high-risk):
> [L60] Tier Count — Excellent (0%)
> [L61] Tier Count — Excellent (1–3%)
> [L62] Tier Count — Low (4–7%)
> [L63] Tier Count — Review (8–9%)
> [L64] Tier Count — Medium (10%+)
> [L65] Tier Count — Medium-High (20%+)
> [L66] Tier Count — High (30%+)
> [L67] 
> [L68] Loan flags:
> [L69] Loan Remaining ≥85% Count
> [L70] 
> [L71] Explicit exclusions (MUST NOT appear):
> [L72] DTI
> [L73] Strategic Notes
> [L74] Total Account Age (yrs)
> [L75] Total Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L76] 
> [L77] ====================
> [L78] C) TSV SCHEMA (ORDERED, EXACT)
> [L79] ====================
> [L80] Bureau\tCreditor Name\tOpening Date\tAge (yrs decimal)\tCredit Limit\tBalance\tUtilization %\tUtilization Tier\tHighest Balance\tOriginal Balance\tAccount Type\tTradeline Type\tResponsibility\tStatus\tStatus Updated Date\tOpen/Closed\tUnderwriting Notes\tAdditional Notes\tFlags
> [L81] 
> [L82] Rules:
> [L83] - Age (yrs+mos) MUST NOT be a TSV column.
> [L84] - Underwriting Notes / Additional Notes blank unless explicitly present.
> [L85] 
> [L86] ====================
> [L87] D) AGE (yrs decimal) — REQUIRED WHEN POSSIBLE
> [L88] ====================
> [L89] - As-of date = report_date
> [L90] - Compute elapsed FULL MONTHS between Opening Date and report_date.
> [L91] - Age (yrs decimal) = months/12 rounded to EXACTLY 4 decimals.
> [L92] - If missing/unparseable dates => Age blank; add AGE_NOT_COMPUTED.
> [L93] 
> [L94] ====================
> [L95] E) UTILIZATION — REVOLVING ONLY (STRICT)
> [L96] ====================
> [L97] - Compute Utilization % and Tier ONLY if Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L98] - For DEPOSIT/OTHER (including overdraft/reserve checking): utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L99] - For INSTALLMENT/LOAN: utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L100] 
> [L101] Utilization %:
> [L102] Balance / Credit Limit * 100, round 2 decimals.
> # 【7†SPEC_CORE.txt†file-UdH7PZmV8pCLCqJ554PU6J】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn4file7
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] Loan flags:
> [L2] Loan Remaining ≥85% Count
> [L3] 
> [L4] Explicit exclusions (MUST NOT appear):
> [L5] DTI
> [L6] Strategic Notes
> [L7] Total Account Age (yrs)
> [L8] Total Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L9] 
> [L10] ====================
> [L11] C) TSV SCHEMA (ORDERED, EXACT)
> [L12] ====================
> [L13] Bureau\tCreditor Name\tOpening Date\tAge (yrs decimal)\tCredit Limit\tBalance\tUtilization %\tUtilization Tier\tHighest Balance\tOriginal Balance\tAccount Type\tTradeline Type\tResponsibility\tStatus\tStatus Updated Date\tOpen/Closed\tUnderwriting Notes\tAdditional Notes\tFlags
> [L14] 
> [L15] Rules:
> [L16] - Age (yrs+mos) MUST NOT be a TSV column.
> [L17] - Underwriting Notes / Additional Notes blank unless explicitly present.
> [L18] 
> [L19] ====================
> [L20] D) AGE (yrs decimal) — REQUIRED WHEN POSSIBLE
> [L21] ====================
> [L22] - As-of date = report_date
> [L23] - Compute elapsed FULL MONTHS between Opening Date and report_date.
> [L24] - Age (yrs decimal) = months/12 rounded to EXACTLY 4 decimals.
> [L25] - If missing/unparseable dates => Age blank; add AGE_NOT_COMPUTED.
> [L26] 
> [L27] ====================
> [L28] E) UTILIZATION — REVOLVING ONLY (STRICT)
> [L29] ====================
> [L30] - Compute Utilization % and Tier ONLY if Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L31] - For DEPOSIT/OTHER (including overdraft/reserve checking): utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L32] - For INSTALLMENT/LOAN: utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L33] 
> [L34] Utilization %:
> [L35] Balance / Credit Limit * 100, round 2 decimals.
> [L36] 
> [L37] Allowed tier labels (EXACT; no numeric ranges like "1–9%" allowed):
> [L38] Excellent (0%)
> [L39] Excellent (1–3%)
> [L40] Low (4–7%)
> [L41] Review (8–9%)
> [L42] Medium (10%+)
> [L43] Medium-High (20%+)
> [L44] High (30%+)
> [L45] 
> [L46] ====================
> [L47] F) ACCOUNT TYPE NORMALIZATION
> [L48] ====================
> [L49] REVOLVING: credit cards, bankcards, LOC.
> [L50] INSTALLMENT/LOAN: student/auto/personal/mortgage loans.
> [L51] DEPOSIT/OTHER: overdraft / reserve checking accounts and similar.
> [L52] 
> [L53] If unclear: blank + AMBIGUOUS_ACCOUNT_TYPE
> [L54] 
> [L55] ====================
> [L56] G) LOAN REMAINING FLAG (LOANS ONLY)
> [L57] ====================
> [L58] Remaining % = Balance / Original Balance (only if Original Balance present and >0)
> [L59] If Remaining % >= 85%: add LOAN_HIGH_REMAINING_85PCT
> [L60] Never apply to REVOLVING or DEPOSIT/OTHER.
> [L61] 
> [L62] ====================
> [L63] H) SORTING (REQUIRED)
> [L64] ====================
> [L65] Sort by Opening Date oldest→newest for parseable dates.
> [L66] Missing/unparseable dates go last (stable) and add OPEN_DATE_UNPARSEABLE.
> # 【8†QA_AND_GATES.txt†file-LsNs8c2SDJcfvZhQBB8JUX】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: QA_AND_GATES.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn4file8
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] QA AND GATES — v2.2 (Contract Gate)
> [L2] 
> [L3] HARD FAIL (FAIL FAST)
> [L4] - unreadable_page
> [L5] - fragmented_row
> [L6] 
> [L7] CONTRACT OUTPUT GATE (HARD FAIL)
> [L8] Fail if ANY are true:
> [L9] - Missing Output A or Output B block
> [L10] - Missing Summary or TSV in either block
> [L11] - TSV header mismatch (must match SPEC_CORE exactly)
> [L12] - Any INQUIRY rows appear inside TSV tables
> [L13] - Age (yrs+mos) appears as TSV column
> [L14] - Any non-blank Age (yrs decimal) not exactly 4 decimals
> [L15] - Any utilization tier on non-REVOLVING rows
> [L16] - Any text outside contract blocks (narration leakage)
> [L17] 
> [L18] QA FAIL FORMAT (ONLY OUTPUT ON FAIL)
> [L19] QA FAIL — <STAGE>
> [L20] Reason	Details
> [L21] <reason>	<details>
> # 【9†QA_AND_GATES.txt†file-CrkACVyC7oc2ktmtAoMZh5】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: QA_AND_GATES.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn4file9
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] QA AND GATES — v2.3 (HARD FAIL ONLY + CONTRACT GATE + RECONCILIATION)
> [L2] 
> [L3] HARD FAIL (FAIL FAST)
> [L4] - unreadable_page
> [L5] - fragmented_row
> [L6] 
> [L7] CONTRACT OUTPUT GATE (HARD FAIL)
> [L8] Fail if ANY are true:
> [L9] (1) Missing either block header:
> [L10]     OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L11]     OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L12] (2) Missing Summary or TSV in either block.
> [L13] (3) TSV header mismatch (must match SPEC_CORE exactly).
> [L14] (4) Summary is not 2-column TAB-separated (e.g., contains "Summary Key Value").
> [L15] (5) Any INQUIRY rows appear inside TSV tables.
> [L16] (6) Any text outside contract blocks (narration leakage).
> [L17] 
> [L18] FIELD FORMAT GATES (HARD FAIL)
> [L19] (7) Any non-blank Age (yrs decimal) is not exactly 4 decimals.
> [L20] (8) Any utilization tier appears on non-REVOLVING rows.
> [L21] (9) Any utilization tier label not in the allowed set.
> [L22] (10) Any DEPOSIT/OTHER row has utilization % or tier non-blank.
> [L23] 
> [L24] RECONCILIATION GATES (HARD FAIL; DEBUGGABLE)
> [L25] Definitions:
> [L26] - A_row_count = number of TSV rows in Output A.
> [L27] - B_row_count = number of TSV rows in Output B.
> [L28] - A_open_count_from_rows = count(Open/Closed == Open) in Output A.
> [L29] - B_open_count_from_rows / B_closed_count_from_rows similarly.
> [L30] - Debt totals computed from TSV rows (numeric parse; blanks ignored).
> [L31] 
> [L32] Fail if ANY are true:
> [L33] (11) Summary key "Accounts Included (This Output)" != row_count for that output.
> [L34] (12) For Output A: Summary "Total Open Accounts" != A_row_count.
> [L35] (13) For Output B: Summary "Total Accounts" != B_row_count.
> [L36] (14) For Output B: Summary "Total Open Accounts" != B_open_count_from_rows.
> [L37] (15) For Output B: Summary "Total Closed Accounts" != B_closed_count_from_rows.
> [L38] (16) Summary "Grand Total Debt" != sum of balances across rows (by numeric parsing).
> [L39] (17) Summary "Total Revolving Debt" != sum of balances where Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L40] (18) Tier counts sum != number of REVOLVING rows with computable utilization.
> [L41] 
> [L42] QA FAIL OUTPUT (ONLY OUTPUT ON FAIL)
> [L43] Format:
> [L44] QA FAIL — <STAGE>
> [L45] Reason\tDetails
> [L46] <reason>\t<details>
> [L47] 
> [L48] DEBUG DETAIL REQUIREMENT
> [L49] - Details MUST include: gate_id, expected, observed, and a fix_hint.
> [L50] - If a row triggers a gate, include a row identifier: "<Creditor Name>|<Opening Date>|<Open/Closed>".

> # 【1†SPEC_CORE.txt†file-RYWNxz9NqmVATC8kcBrYYv】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn5file1
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] SPEC CORE — v2.2 (Contract)
> [L2] 
> [L3] TSV HEADER (EXACT)
> [L4] Bureau	Creditor Name	Opening Date	Age (yrs decimal)	Credit Limit	Balance	Utilization %	Utilization Tier	Highest Balance	Original Balance	Account Type	Tradeline Type	Responsibility	Status	Status Updated Date	Open/Closed	Underwriting Notes	Additional Notes	Flags
> [L5] 
> [L6] RULES (KEY)
> [L7] - Age (yrs+mos) is SUMMARY-ONLY.
> [L8] - Age (yrs decimal) = full months / 12, EXACTLY 4 decimals, as-of report_date.
> [L9] - Sort by Opening Date oldest→newest; missing/unparseable last (stable).
> [L10] - Utilization + tiers ONLY for REVOLVING; loans must have util blanks.
> [L11] - Loan Remaining >=85% (Balance/Original Balance) => LOAN_HIGH_REMAINING_85PCT (loans only).
> [L12] - Do NOT dedupe; flag DUPLICATE_REVIEW if suspected.
> # 【2†MASTER_INDEX.txt†file-889aKgioqc2WDHFBCLf6ZU】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: MASTER_INDEX.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn5file2
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] MASTER INDEX — Credit Report TSV Extractor
> [L2] Spec Version: v2.3_CONTRACT_DEBUGGABLE
> [L3] Checklist Authority: MASTER_CHECKLIST_FINAL_v3
> [L4] 
> [L5] MISSION
> [L6] Produce contract-compliant, Google-Sheets-ready outputs:
> [L7] 
> [L8] OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L9] - Summary (2 columns, TAB-separated)
> [L10] - TSV (TAB-separated; exact header)
> [L11] 
> [L12] OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L13] - Summary (2 columns, TAB-separated)
> [L14] - TSV (TAB-separated; exact header)
> [L15] 
> [L16] FILE PRECEDENCE (HIGHEST→LOWEST)
> [L17] 1) SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS.txt
> [L18] 2) MASTER_INDEX.txt
> [L19] 3) SPEC_CORE.txt
> [L20] 4) QA_AND_GATES.txt
> [L21] 5) COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST.txt
> [L22] 
> [L23] EXECUTION FLOW (DETERMINISTIC)
> [L24] 1) Extract metadata:
> [L25]    - bureau
> [L26]    - report_date (as-of date) REQUIRED for age calculations
> [L27]    - fico score if present (summary only)
> [L28] 2) Parse tradelines (ACCOUNT rows only). Never include INQUIRY in tradeline TSV.
> [L29] 3) Normalize Account Type:
> [L30]    - REVOLVING (credit card/LOC)
> [L31]    - INSTALLMENT/LOAN
> [L32]    - DEPOSIT/OTHER (e.g., overdraft/reserve checking)
> [L33] 4) Compute derived fields per SPEC_CORE.
> [L34] 5) Build Output A (Open only) and Output B (Open+Closed).
> [L35] 6) Sort within each output by Opening Date oldest→newest; missing/unparseable last (stable).
> [L36] 7) Build Summary blocks (2 columns, tab-separated) per SPEC_CORE required keys.
> [L37] 8) Run QA gates (including reconciliation gates). If any fail => QA FAIL with details + fix hints.
> [L38] 9) Output:
> [L39]    - PASS => Output A block then Output B block (no extra text)
> [L40]    - FAIL => QA FAIL block only

> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn5file3
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] You are a silent, underwriting-grade credit report extractor.
> [L2] 
> [L3] OUTPUT CONTRACT (HARD)
> [L4] - Output ONLY the contract blocks (Output A then Output B), OR output ONLY a QA FAIL block.
> [L5] - Never output narration, markdown, or explanations outside QA FAIL.
> [L6] 
> [L7] DEBUGGABILITY (ON FAIL)
> [L8] - When failing, QA FAIL must include full actionable details:
> [L9]   - which gate failed
> [L10]   - which rows/fields triggered it (as identifiers)
> [L11]   - expected vs observed
> [L12]   - a concrete fix hint
> [L13] 
> [L14] PERFORMANCE
> [L15] - No long deliberation loops.
> [L16] - If scanned/unreadable/fragmented => FAIL FAST (<10s) with QA FAIL.
> # 【4†SPEC_CORE.txt†file-UdH7PZmV8pCLCqJ554PU6J】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn5file4
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] Loan flags:
> [L2] Loan Remaining ≥85% Count
> [L3] 
> [L4] Explicit exclusions (MUST NOT appear):
> [L5] DTI
> [L6] Strategic Notes
> [L7] Total Account Age (yrs)
> [L8] Total Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L9] 
> [L10] ====================
> [L11] C) TSV SCHEMA (ORDERED, EXACT)
> [L12] ====================
> [L13] Bureau\tCreditor Name\tOpening Date\tAge (yrs decimal)\tCredit Limit\tBalance\tUtilization %\tUtilization Tier\tHighest Balance\tOriginal Balance\tAccount Type\tTradeline Type\tResponsibility\tStatus\tStatus Updated Date\tOpen/Closed\tUnderwriting Notes\tAdditional Notes\tFlags
> [L14] 
> [L15] Rules:
> [L16] - Age (yrs+mos) MUST NOT be a TSV column.
> [L17] - Underwriting Notes / Additional Notes blank unless explicitly present.
> [L18] 
> [L19] ====================
> [L20] D) AGE (yrs decimal) — REQUIRED WHEN POSSIBLE
> [L21] ====================
> [L22] - As-of date = report_date
> [L23] - Compute elapsed FULL MONTHS between Opening Date and report_date.
> [L24] - Age (yrs decimal) = months/12 rounded to EXACTLY 4 decimals.
> [L25] - If missing/unparseable dates => Age blank; add AGE_NOT_COMPUTED.
> [L26] 
> [L27] ====================
> [L28] E) UTILIZATION — REVOLVING ONLY (STRICT)
> [L29] ====================
> [L30] - Compute Utilization % and Tier ONLY if Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L31] - For DEPOSIT/OTHER (including overdraft/reserve checking): utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L32] - For INSTALLMENT/LOAN: utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L33] 
> [L34] Utilization %:
> [L35] Balance / Credit Limit * 100, round 2 decimals.
> [L36] 
> [L37] Allowed tier labels (EXACT; no numeric ranges like "1–9%" allowed):
> [L38] Excellent (0%)
> [L39] Excellent (1–3%)
> [L40] Low (4–7%)
> [L41] Review (8–9%)
> [L42] Medium (10%+)
> [L43] Medium-High (20%+)
> [L44] High (30%+)
> [L45] 
> [L46] ====================
> [L47] F) ACCOUNT TYPE NORMALIZATION
> [L48] ====================
> [L49] REVOLVING: credit cards, bankcards, LOC.
> [L50] INSTALLMENT/LOAN: student/auto/personal/mortgage loans.
> [L51] DEPOSIT/OTHER: overdraft / reserve checking accounts and similar.
> [L52] 
> [L53] If unclear: blank + AMBIGUOUS_ACCOUNT_TYPE
> [L54] 
> [L55] ====================
> [L56] G) LOAN REMAINING FLAG (LOANS ONLY)
> [L57] ====================
> [L58] Remaining % = Balance / Original Balance (only if Original Balance present and >0)
> [L59] If Remaining % >= 85%: add LOAN_HIGH_REMAINING_85PCT
> [L60] Never apply to REVOLVING or DEPOSIT/OTHER.
> [L61] 
> [L62] ====================
> [L63] H) SORTING (REQUIRED)
> [L64] ====================
> [L65] Sort by Opening Date oldest→newest for parseable dates.
> [L66] Missing/unparseable dates go last (stable) and add OPEN_DATE_UNPARSEABLE.

> Content source: Source.file
> Title: QA_AND_GATES.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn5file5
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] QA AND GATES — v2.3 (HARD FAIL ONLY + CONTRACT GATE + RECONCILIATION)
> [L2] 
> [L3] HARD FAIL (FAIL FAST)
> [L4] - unreadable_page
> [L5] - fragmented_row
> [L6] 
> [L7] CONTRACT OUTPUT GATE (HARD FAIL)
> [L8] Fail if ANY are true:
> [L9] (1) Missing either block header:
> [L10]     OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L11]     OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L12] (2) Missing Summary or TSV in either block.
> [L13] (3) TSV header mismatch (must match SPEC_CORE exactly).
> [L14] (4) Summary is not 2-column TAB-separated (e.g., contains "Summary Key Value").
> [L15] (5) Any INQUIRY rows appear inside TSV tables.
> [L16] (6) Any text outside contract blocks (narration leakage).
> [L17] 
> [L18] FIELD FORMAT GATES (HARD FAIL)
> [L19] (7) Any non-blank Age (yrs decimal) is not exactly 4 decimals.
> [L20] (8) Any utilization tier appears on non-REVOLVING rows.
> [L21] (9) Any utilization tier label not in the allowed set.
> [L22] (10) Any DEPOSIT/OTHER row has utilization % or tier non-blank.
> [L23] 
> [L24] RECONCILIATION GATES (HARD FAIL; DEBUGGABLE)
> [L25] Definitions:
> [L26] - A_row_count = number of TSV rows in Output A.
> [L27] - B_row_count = number of TSV rows in Output B.
> [L28] - A_open_count_from_rows = count(Open/Closed == Open) in Output A.
> [L29] - B_open_count_from_rows / B_closed_count_from_rows similarly.
> [L30] - Debt totals computed from TSV rows (numeric parse; blanks ignored).
> [L31] 
> [L32] Fail if ANY are true:
> [L33] (11) Summary key "Accounts Included (This Output)" != row_count for that output.
> [L34] (12) For Output A: Summary "Total Open Accounts" != A_row_count.
> [L35] (13) For Output B: Summary "Total Accounts" != B_row_count.
> [L36] (14) For Output B: Summary "Total Open Accounts" != B_open_count_from_rows.
> [L37] (15) For Output B: Summary "Total Closed Accounts" != B_closed_count_from_rows.
> [L38] (16) Summary "Grand Total Debt" != sum of balances across rows (by numeric parsing).
> [L39] (17) Summary "Total Revolving Debt" != sum of balances where Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L40] (18) Tier counts sum != number of REVOLVING rows with computable utilization.
> [L41] 
> [L42] QA FAIL OUTPUT (ONLY OUTPUT ON FAIL)
> [L43] Format:
> [L44] QA FAIL — <STAGE>
> [L45] Reason\tDetails
> [L46] <reason>\t<details>
> [L47] 
> [L48] DEBUG DETAIL REQUIREMENT
> [L49] - Details MUST include: gate_id, expected, observed, and a fix_hint.
> [L50] - If a row triggers a gate, include a row identifier: "<Creditor Name>|<Opening Date>|<Open/Closed>".

> [L1] SPEC CORE — v2.2 (Contract)
> [L2] 
> [L3] TSV HEADER (EXACT)
> [L4] Bureau	Creditor Name	Opening Date	Age (yrs decimal)	Credit Limit	Balance	Utilization %	Utilization Tier	Highest Balance	Original Balance	Account Type	Tradeline Type	Responsibility	Status	Status Updated Date	Open/Closed	Underwriting Notes	Additional Notes	Flags
> [L5] 
> [L6] RULES (KEY)
> [L7] - Age (yrs+mos) is SUMMARY-ONLY.
> [L8] - Age (yrs decimal) = full months / 12, EXACTLY 4 decimals, as-of report_date.
> [L9] - Sort by Opening Date oldest→newest; missing/unparseable last (stable).
> [L10] - Utilization + tiers ONLY for REVOLVING; loans must have util blanks.
> [L11] - Loan Remaining >=85% (Balance/Original Balance) => LOAN_HIGH_REMAINING_85PCT (loans only).
> [L12] - Do NOT dedupe; flag DUPLICATE_REVIEW if suspected.

> Content source: Source.file
> Title: MASTER_INDEX.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn6file3
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] MASTER INDEX — Credit Report TSV Extractor
> [L2] Spec Version: v2.2_FAILSAFE_INDEX_NAMES
> [L3] 
> [L4] MISSION
> [L5] Produce contract-compliant outputs:
> [L6] 
> [L7] OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L8] - Summary (2 columns, tab-separated)
> [L9] - TSV (tab-separated; exact header)
> [L10] 
> [L11] OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L12] - Summary (2 columns, tab-separated)
> [L13] - TSV (tab-separated; exact header)
> [L14] 
> [L15] FILE PRECEDENCE (HIGHEST→LOWEST)
> [L16] 1) SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS.txt
> [L17] 2) MASTER_INDEX.txt (or MASTER_INDEX.txt)
> [L18] 3) SPEC_CORE.txt
> [L19] 4) QA_AND_GATES.txt
> [L20] 5) COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST.txt
> [L21] 
> [L22] EXECUTION FLOW
> [L23] 1) Read MASTER_INDEX.
> [L24] 2) Parse metadata: bureau, report_date (as-of), fico if present.
> [L25] 3) Parse tradelines: ACCOUNT rows only; never mix INQUIRY into tradeline TSV.
> [L26] 4) Derive fields; split Output A/B; sort; build summaries.
> [L27] 5) Run CONTRACT OUTPUT GATE. If fail => QA FAIL only.
> # 【4†MASTER_INDEX.txt†file-889aKgioqc2WDHFBCLf6ZU】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: MASTER_INDEX.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn6file4
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] MASTER INDEX — Credit Report TSV Extractor
> [L2] Spec Version: v2.3_CONTRACT_DEBUGGABLE
> [L3] Checklist Authority: MASTER_CHECKLIST_FINAL_v3
> [L4] 
> [L5] MISSION
> [L6] Produce contract-compliant, Google-Sheets-ready outputs:
> [L7] 
> [L8] OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L9] - Summary (2 columns, TAB-separated)
> [L10] - TSV (TAB-separated; exact header)
> [L11] 
> [L12] OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L13] - Summary (2 columns, TAB-separated)
> [L14] - TSV (TAB-separated; exact header)
> [L15] 
> [L16] FILE PRECEDENCE (HIGHEST→LOWEST)
> [L17] 1) SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS.txt
> [L18] 2) MASTER_INDEX.txt
> [L19] 3) SPEC_CORE.txt
> [L20] 4) QA_AND_GATES.txt
> [L21] 5) COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST.txt
> [L22] 
> [L23] EXECUTION FLOW (DETERMINISTIC)
> [L24] 1) Extract metadata:
> [L25]    - bureau
> [L26]    - report_date (as-of date) REQUIRED for age calculations
> [L27]    - fico score if present (summary only)
> [L28] 2) Parse tradelines (ACCOUNT rows only). Never include INQUIRY in tradeline TSV.
> [L29] 3) Normalize Account Type:
> [L30]    - REVOLVING (credit card/LOC)
> [L31]    - INSTALLMENT/LOAN
> [L32]    - DEPOSIT/OTHER (e.g., overdraft/reserve checking)
> [L33] 4) Compute derived fields per SPEC_CORE.
> [L34] 5) Build Output A (Open only) and Output B (Open+Closed).
> [L35] 6) Sort within each output by Opening Date oldest→newest; missing/unparseable last (stable).
> [L36] 7) Build Summary blocks (2 columns, tab-separated) per SPEC_CORE required keys.
> [L37] 8) Run QA gates (including reconciliation gates). If any fail => QA FAIL with details + fix hints.
> [L38] 9) Output:
> [L39]    - PASS => Output A block then Output B block (no extra text)
> [L40]    - FAIL => QA FAIL block only
> # 【5†SPEC_CORE.txt†file-UdH7PZmV8pCLCqJ554PU6J】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn6file5
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] SPEC CORE — v2.3 (Contract + Audit-Friendly)
> [L2] 
> [L3] ====================
> [L4] A) OUTPUT BLOCK FORMAT (EXACT)
> [L5] ====================
> [L6] OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L7] <Summary A: 2 columns, TAB-separated; one key per line>
> [L8] <blank line>
> [L9] <TSV A: header row + rows, TAB-separated>
> [L10] 
> [L11] <blank line>
> [L12] OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L13] <Summary B: 2 columns, TAB-separated; one key per line>
> [L14] <blank line>
> [L15] <TSV B: header row + rows, TAB-separated>
> [L16] 
> [L17] No other text is allowed.
> [L18] 
> [L19] ====================
> [L20] B) SUMMARY (2 COLUMNS, TAB-SEPARATED; REQUIRED KEYS)
> [L21] ====================
> [L22] - Each summary line MUST be: <Key>\t<Value>
> [L23] - No header row like "Summary Key Value" (forbidden).
> [L24] 
> [L25] Bureau label (TU):
> [L26] - TRANSUNION SUMMARY (OPEN ONLY)
> [L27] - TRANSUNION SUMMARY (OPEN + CLOSED)
> [L28] 
> [L29] Required keys (appear exactly once; blank value allowed):
> [L30] Report Date
> [L31] Bureau
> [L32] FICO Score 8
> [L33] 
> [L34] Counts:
> [L35] Total Open Accounts
> [L36] Total Closed Accounts
> [L37] Total Accounts
> [L38] Accounts Included (This Output)
> [L39] 
> [L40] Ages:
> [L41] Average Account Age (yrs decimal)   (4dp)
> [L42] Average Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L43] Oldest Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L44] Newest Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L45] 
> [L46] Debt totals (numeric or blank):
> [L47] Grand Total Debt
> [L48] Total Revolving Debt
> [L49] Total Auto Loan Debt
> [L50] Total Personal Loan Debt
> [L51] Total Student Loan Debt
> [L52] Total Mortgage Debt
> [L53] Total Other Debt
> [L54] 
> [L55] Revolving aggregates:
> [L56] Total Revolving Credit Limits
> [L57] Overall Revolving Utilization %
> [L58] 
> [L59] Tier counts (ONLY for REVOLVING; ordered low-risk → high-risk):
> [L60] Tier Count — Excellent (0%)
> [L61] Tier Count — Excellent (1–3%)
> [L62] Tier Count — Low (4–7%)
> [L63] Tier Count — Review (8–9%)
> [L64] Tier Count — Medium (10%+)
> [L65] Tier Count — Medium-High (20%+)
> [L66] Tier Count — High (30%+)
> [L67] 
> [L68] Loan flags:
> [L69] Loan Remaining ≥85% Count
> [L70] 
> [L71] Explicit exclusions (MUST NOT appear):
> [L72] DTI
> [L73] Strategic Notes
> [L74] Total Account Age (yrs)
> [L75] Total Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L76] 
> [L77] ====================
> [L78] C) TSV SCHEMA (ORDERED, EXACT)
> [L79] ====================
> [L80] Bureau\tCreditor Name\tOpening Date\tAge (yrs decimal)\tCredit Limit\tBalance\tUtilization %\tUtilization Tier\tHighest Balance\tOriginal Balance\tAccount Type\tTradeline Type\tResponsibility\tStatus\tStatus Updated Date\tOpen/Closed\tUnderwriting Notes\tAdditional Notes\tFlags
> [L81] 
> [L82] Rules:
> [L83] - Age (yrs+mos) MUST NOT be a TSV column.
> [L84] - Underwriting Notes / Additional Notes blank unless explicitly present.
> [L85] 
> [L86] ====================
> [L87] D) AGE (yrs decimal) — REQUIRED WHEN POSSIBLE
> [L88] ====================
> [L89] - As-of date = report_date
> [L90] - Compute elapsed FULL MONTHS between Opening Date and report_date.
> [L91] - Age (yrs decimal) = months/12 rounded to EXACTLY 4 decimals.
> [L92] - If missing/unparseable dates => Age blank; add AGE_NOT_COMPUTED.
> [L93] 
> [L94] ====================
> [L95] E) UTILIZATION — REVOLVING ONLY (STRICT)
> [L96] ====================
> [L97] - Compute Utilization % and Tier ONLY if Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L98] - For DEPOSIT/OTHER (including overdraft/reserve checking): utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L99] - For INSTALLMENT/LOAN: utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L100] 
> [L101] Utilization %:
> [L102] Balance / Credit Limit * 100, round 2 decimals.

> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn6file7
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] Loan flags:
> [L2] Loan Remaining ≥85% Count
> [L3] 
> [L4] Explicit exclusions (MUST NOT appear):
> [L5] DTI
> [L6] Strategic Notes
> [L7] Total Account Age (yrs)
> [L8] Total Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L9] 
> [L10] ====================
> [L11] C) TSV SCHEMA (ORDERED, EXACT)
> [L12] ====================
> [L13] Bureau\tCreditor Name\tOpening Date\tAge (yrs decimal)\tCredit Limit\tBalance\tUtilization %\tUtilization Tier\tHighest Balance\tOriginal Balance\tAccount Type\tTradeline Type\tResponsibility\tStatus\tStatus Updated Date\tOpen/Closed\tUnderwriting Notes\tAdditional Notes\tFlags
> [L14] 
> [L15] Rules:
> [L16] - Age (yrs+mos) MUST NOT be a TSV column.
> [L17] - Underwriting Notes / Additional Notes blank unless explicitly present.
> [L18] 
> [L19] ====================
> [L20] D) AGE (yrs decimal) — REQUIRED WHEN POSSIBLE
> [L21] ====================
> [L22] - As-of date = report_date
> [L23] - Compute elapsed FULL MONTHS between Opening Date and report_date.
> [L24] - Age (yrs decimal) = months/12 rounded to EXACTLY 4 decimals.
> [L25] - If missing/unparseable dates => Age blank; add AGE_NOT_COMPUTED.
> [L26] 
> [L27] ====================
> [L28] E) UTILIZATION — REVOLVING ONLY (STRICT)
> [L29] ====================
> [L30] - Compute Utilization % and Tier ONLY if Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L31] - For DEPOSIT/OTHER (including overdraft/reserve checking): utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L32] - For INSTALLMENT/LOAN: utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L33] 
> [L34] Utilization %:
> [L35] Balance / Credit Limit * 100, round 2 decimals.
> [L36] 
> [L37] Allowed tier labels (EXACT; no numeric ranges like "1–9%" allowed):
> [L38] Excellent (0%)
> [L39] Excellent (1–3%)
> [L40] Low (4–7%)
> [L41] Review (8–9%)
> [L42] Medium (10%+)
> [L43] Medium-High (20%+)
> [L44] High (30%+)
> [L45] 
> [L46] ====================
> [L47] F) ACCOUNT TYPE NORMALIZATION
> [L48] ====================
> [L49] REVOLVING: credit cards, bankcards, LOC.
> [L50] INSTALLMENT/LOAN: student/auto/personal/mortgage loans.
> [L51] DEPOSIT/OTHER: overdraft / reserve checking accounts and similar.
> [L52] 
> [L53] If unclear: blank + AMBIGUOUS_ACCOUNT_TYPE
> [L54] 
> [L55] ====================
> [L56] G) LOAN REMAINING FLAG (LOANS ONLY)
> [L57] ====================
> [L58] Remaining % = Balance / Original Balance (only if Original Balance present and >0)
> [L59] If Remaining % >= 85%: add LOAN_HIGH_REMAINING_85PCT
> [L60] Never apply to REVOLVING or DEPOSIT/OTHER.
> [L61] 
> [L62] ====================
> [L63] H) SORTING (REQUIRED)
> [L64] ====================
> [L65] Sort by Opening Date oldest→newest for parseable dates.
> [L66] Missing/unparseable dates go last (stable) and add OPEN_DATE_UNPARSEABLE.

> # 【14†QA_AND_GATES.txt†file-CrkACVyC7oc2ktmtAoMZh5】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: QA_AND_GATES.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn6file14
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] QA AND GATES — v2.3 (HARD FAIL ONLY + CONTRACT GATE + RECONCILIATION)
> [L2] 
> [L3] HARD FAIL (FAIL FAST)
> [L4] - unreadable_page
> [L5] - fragmented_row
> [L6] 
> [L7] CONTRACT OUTPUT GATE (HARD FAIL)
> [L8] Fail if ANY are true:
> [L9] (1) Missing either block header:
> [L10]     OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L11]     OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L12] (2) Missing Summary or TSV in either block.
> [L13] (3) TSV header mismatch (must match SPEC_CORE exactly).
> [L14] (4) Summary is not 2-column TAB-separated (e.g., contains "Summary Key Value").
> [L15] (5) Any INQUIRY rows appear inside TSV tables.
> [L16] (6) Any text outside contract blocks (narration leakage).
> [L17] 
> [L18] FIELD FORMAT GATES (HARD FAIL)
> [L19] (7) Any non-blank Age (yrs decimal) is not exactly 4 decimals.
> [L20] (8) Any utilization tier appears on non-REVOLVING rows.
> [L21] (9) Any utilization tier label not in the allowed set.
> [L22] (10) Any DEPOSIT/OTHER row has utilization % or tier non-blank.
> [L23] 
> [L24] RECONCILIATION GATES (HARD FAIL; DEBUGGABLE)
> [L25] Definitions:
> [L26] - A_row_count = number of TSV rows in Output A.
> [L27] - B_row_count = number of TSV rows in Output B.
> [L28] - A_open_count_from_rows = count(Open/Closed == Open) in Output A.
> [L29] - B_open_count_from_rows / B_closed_count_from_rows similarly.
> [L30] - Debt totals computed from TSV rows (numeric parse; blanks ignored).
> [L31] 
> [L32] Fail if ANY are true:
> [L33] (11) Summary key "Accounts Included (This Output)" != row_count for that output.
> [L34] (12) For Output A: Summary "Total Open Accounts" != A_row_count.
> [L35] (13) For Output B: Summary "Total Accounts" != B_row_count.
> [L36] (14) For Output B: Summary "Total Open Accounts" != B_open_count_from_rows.
> [L37] (15) For Output B: Summary "Total Closed Accounts" != B_closed_count_from_rows.
> [L38] (16) Summary "Grand Total Debt" != sum of balances across rows (by numeric parsing).
> [L39] (17) Summary "Total Revolving Debt" != sum of balances where Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L40] (18) Tier counts sum != number of REVOLVING rows with computable utilization.
> [L41] 
> [L42] QA FAIL OUTPUT (ONLY OUTPUT ON FAIL)
> [L43] Format:
> [L44] QA FAIL — <STAGE>
> [L45] Reason\tDetails
> [L46] <reason>\t<details>
> [L47] 
> [L48] DEBUG DETAIL REQUIREMENT
> [L49] - Details MUST include: gate_id, expected, observed, and a fix_hint.
> [L50] - If a row triggers a gate, include a row identifier: "<Creditor Name>|<Opening Date>|<Open/Closed>".

> # 【2†SPEC_CORE.txt†file-RYWNxz9NqmVATC8kcBrYYv】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn7file2
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] SPEC CORE — v2.2 (Contract)
> [L2] 
> [L3] TSV HEADER (EXACT)
> [L4] Bureau	Creditor Name	Opening Date	Age (yrs decimal)	Credit Limit	Balance	Utilization %	Utilization Tier	Highest Balance	Original Balance	Account Type	Tradeline Type	Responsibility	Status	Status Updated Date	Open/Closed	Underwriting Notes	Additional Notes	Flags
> [L5] 
> [L6] RULES (KEY)
> [L7] - Age (yrs+mos) is SUMMARY-ONLY.
> [L8] - Age (yrs decimal) = full months / 12, EXACTLY 4 decimals, as-of report_date.
> [L9] - Sort by Opening Date oldest→newest; missing/unparseable last (stable).
> [L10] - Utilization + tiers ONLY for REVOLVING; loans must have util blanks.
> [L11] - Loan Remaining >=85% (Balance/Original Balance) => LOAN_HIGH_REMAINING_85PCT (loans only).
> [L12] - Do NOT dedupe; flag DUPLICATE_REVIEW if suspected.
